    \newsavebox\pandoc@box
    \newcommand*\pandocbounded[1]{%
      \sbox\pandoc@box{#1}%
      % scaling factors for width and height
      \Gscale@div\@tempa\textheight{\dimexpr\ht\pandoc@box+\dp\pandoc@box\relax}%
      \Gscale@div\@tempb\linewidth{\wd\pandoc@box}%
      % select the smaller of both
      \ifdim\@tempb\p@<\@tempa\p@
        \let\@tempa\@tempb
      \fi
      % scaling accordingly (\@tempa < 1)
      \ifdim\@tempa\p@<\p@
        \scalebox{\@tempa}{\usebox\pandoc@box}%
      % scaling not needed, use as it is
      \else
        \usebox{\pandoc@box}%
      \fi
    }

\def\PY@reset{\let\PY@it=\relax \let\PY@bf=\relax%
    \let\PY@ul=\relax \let\PY@tc=\relax%
    \let\PY@bc=\relax \let\PY@ff=\relax}
\def\PY@tok#1{\csname PY@tok@#1\endcsname}
\def\PY@toks#1+{\ifx\relax#1\empty\else%
    \PY@tok{#1}\expandafter\PY@toks\fi}
\def\PY@do#1{\PY@bc{\PY@tc{\PY@ul{%
    \PY@it{\PY@bf{\PY@ff{#1}}}}}}}
\def\PY#1#2{\PY@reset\PY@toks#1+\relax+\PY@do{#2}}

    % For linebreaks inside Verbatim environment from package fancyvrb.
    \makeatletter
        \newbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox
        \newbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox
        \newcommand*\Wrappedvisiblespace {\textcolor{red}{\textvisiblespace}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol {\textcolor{red}{\llap{\tiny$\m@th\hookrightarrow$}}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationindent {3ex }
        \newcommand*\Wrappedafterbreak {\kern\Wrappedcontinuationindent\copy\Wrappedcontinuationbox}
        % Take advantage of the already applied Pygments mark-up to insert
        % potential linebreaks for TeX processing.
        %        {, <, #, %, $, ' and ": go to next line.
        %        _, }, ^, &, >, - and ~: stay at end of broken line.
        % Use of \textquotesingle for straight quote.
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatspecials {%
            \def\PYGZus{\discretionary{\char`\_}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\_}}%
            \def\PYGZob{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\{}{\char`\{}}%
            \def\PYGZcb{\discretionary{\char`\}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\}}}%
            \def\PYGZca{\discretionary{\char`\^}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\^}}%
            \def\PYGZam{\discretionary{\char`\&}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\&}}%
            \def\PYGZlt{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\<}{\char`\<}}%
            \def\PYGZgt{\discretionary{\char`\>}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\>}}%
            \def\PYGZsh{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\#}{\char`\#}}%
            \def\PYGZpc{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\%}{\char`\%}}%
            \def\PYGZdl{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\$}{\char`\$}}%
            \def\PYGZhy{\discretionary{\char`\-}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\-}}%
            \def\PYGZsq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\textquotesingle}{\textquotesingle}}%
            \def\PYGZdq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\"}{\char`\"}}%
            \def\PYGZti{\discretionary{\char`\~}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\~}}%
        }
        % Some characters . , ; ? ! / are not pygmentized.
        % This macro makes them "active" and they will insert potential linebreaks
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatpunct {%
            \lccode`\~`\.\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\.}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\.}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\,\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\,}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\,}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\;\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\;}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\;}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\:\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\:}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\:}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\?\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\?}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\?}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\!\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\!}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\!}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\/\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\/}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\/}}}%
            \catcode`\.\active
            \catcode`\,\active
            \catcode`\;\active
            \catcode`\:\active
            \catcode`\?\active
            \catcode`\!\active
            \catcode`\/\active
            \lccode`\~`\~
        }
    \makeatother

    \let\OriginalVerbatim=\Verbatim
    \makeatletter
    \renewcommand{\Verbatim}[1][1]{%
        %\parskip\z@skip
        \sbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox {\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol}%
        \sbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox {\FV@SetupFont\Wrappedvisiblespace}%
        \def\FancyVerbFormatLine ##1{\hsize\linewidth
            \vtop{\raggedright\hyphenpenalty\z@\exhyphenpenalty\z@
                \doublehyphendemerits\z@\finalhyphendemerits\z@
                \strut ##1\strut}%
        }%
        % If the linebreak is at a space, the latter will be displayed as visible
        % space at end of first line, and a continuation symbol starts next line.
        % Stretch/shrink are however usually zero for typewriter font.
        \def\FV@Space {%
            \nobreak\hskip\z@ plus\fontdimen3\font minus\fontdimen4\font
            \discretionary{\copy\Wrappedvisiblespacebox}{\Wrappedafterbreak}
            {\kern\fontdimen2\font}%
        }%

        % Allow breaks at special characters using \PYG... macros.
        \Wrappedbreaksatspecials
        % Breaks at punctuation characters . , ; ? ! and / need catcode=\active
        \OriginalVerbatim[#1,codes*=\Wrappedbreaksatpunct]%
    }
    \makeatother

    r≈−0,76. Pela escala que você está usando, isso indica uma correlação
linear forte e negativa: neste grupo, tempos de reação maiores tendem a
acompanhar valores menores de acuidade visual.

    Depois de um treinamento, seis operários submeteram-se a um teste e,
mais tarde, mediu-se a produtividade de cada um deles. A partir dos
resultados apresentados na tabela abaixo, calcule o coeficiente de
correlação entre a nota do teste e a produtividade. O que podemos aferir
desse resultado? Calcule também a assimetria da amostra teste e da
amostra produtividade e compare-as.

    \paragraph{Terceira Lista.}\label{terceira-lista.}

1 - Ajustar uma reta de mínimos quadrados aos dados da tabela abaixo,
adotando-se:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  X com variável independente;\\
\item
  X como variável dependente;\\
\item
  Representar, graficamente, as duas retas obtidas.\\
\item
  Calcule o grau de ajuste de ambas as retas
\end{itemize}

Cálculo de \(\beta\).

\[
        \beta = \frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i y_i - (n\cdot\bar{x}\cdot\bar{y})}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_i^2) - (\bar{x}^2)}
\]

\[
\alpha = \bar{y}-\beta\cdot \bar{x}
\]

\[
X = [1, 3, 4, 6, 8, 9, 11, 14]\\
Y = [1, 2, 4, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9]
\]

    \paragraph{\texorpdfstring{\(R^2\) dos itens
acima.}{R\^{}2 dos itens acima.}}\label{r2-dos-itens-acima.}

Lembrando que:

\[
r = \mathrm{\frac{cov}{s_x\cdot s_y}}
\]

\[
\mathrm{cov(xy)=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n\;(x_i-\bar{x})(y_i - \bar{y})}{n}}
\]

e

\[
{s_x=\sqrt{\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n(x_i - \bar{x})^2}{n}}}
\]

então usando os dados do primeiro item temos:

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{56}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Grau de ajuste}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} X como variável independente.}

    2 - Ajustar uma reta de mínimos quadrados aos dados da tabela abaixo,
adotando: - X como variável independente; - X como variável dependente;
- Em um mesmo sistema de eixos, desenhar os gráficos das duas retas -
Avaliar a altura de um estudante, cujo peso conhecido é de 63 kg -
Avaliar o peso de um estudante, cuja altura conhecida é de 168 cm -
Calcule o grau de ajuste de ambas as regressões

    Cálculo de \(\beta\):

\[
\beta =
\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i y_i - n\bar{x}\bar{y}}
     {\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i^2 - n\bar{x}^{2}}
\]

Cálculo de \(\alpha\):

\[
\alpha = \bar{y} - \beta\bar{x}
\]

    3 - A produção de aço de um país, em milhões de toneladas (1
tonelada=1000 kg), durante os anos de 1994 a 2004, está indicada na
tabela abaixo.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{(\alph{enumi})}
\tightlist
\item
  Representar graficamente os dados.\\
\item
  Determinar a regressão linear que se ajuste aos dados.\\
\item
  Avaliar a produção de aço, durante os anos de 2003 e 2004, e
  compará-la com os valores reais, de 112,7 e 85,3 milhões de toneladas,
  respectivamente.\\
\item
  Avaliar a produção de aço, durante os anos de 1990 e 1991, e
  compará-la com os valores reais, de 79,7 e 89,6 milhões de toneladas;
  respectivamente.
\end{enumerate}

\[
    \beta = \frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i y_i - (n\cdot\bar{x}\cdot\bar{y})}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_i^2) - (\bar{x}^2)}
\]

\[
\alpha = \bar{y}-\beta\cdot \bar{x}
\]

Lembrando que o coeficiente de Pearson é:

\[
r = \mathrm{\frac{cov}{s_x\cdot s_y}}
\]

\[
\mathrm{cov(xy)=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n\;(x_i-\bar{x})(y_i - \bar{y})}{n}}
\]

e a covariância e o desvio padrão são:

\[
{s_x=\sqrt{\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n(x_i - \bar{x})^2}{n}}}
\]

    Calculo de \(R^2\)

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Período}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodo}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}
\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Produção}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodo}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}
\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx x DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{n}{cov} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx x DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodo} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}producao}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{/}\PY{n}{n}
\PY{n}{sx} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{/}\PY{n}{n}
\PY{n}{sy} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{/}\PY{n}{n}
\PY{n}{r} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{cov}\PY{o}{/}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{sx}\PY{o}{*}\PY{n}{sy}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{r2} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{r}\PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{COV: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{cov}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s1}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Desvio padrão do Período: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{sx}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s1}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Desvio padrão da Produção: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{sy}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s1}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{interpretar\PYZus{}r2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{r2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Período}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Produção}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}


\PY{n}{mostrar\PYZus{}totais} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Período}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Produção}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{DMqx x DMqy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{p}{)}

    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    MMQ \(\beta\) e \(\alpha\) \[
    \beta = \frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i y_i - (n\cdot\bar{x}\cdot\bar{y})}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_i^2) - (\bar{x}^2)}
\]

\[
\alpha = \bar{y}-\beta\cdot \bar{x}
\]

Lembrando que o coeficiente de Pearson é:

\[
r = \mathrm{\frac{cov}{s_x\cdot s_y}}
\]

\[
\mathrm{cov(xy)=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n\;(x_i-\bar{x})(y_i - \bar{y})}{n}}
\]

e a covariância e o desvio padrão são:

\[
{s_x=\sqrt{\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n(x_i - \bar{x})^2}{n}}}
\]

Para ajustar a reta:

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{64}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos x Unidades Produzidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Unidades Produzidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{n}{mostrar\PYZus{}totais}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Unidades Produzidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos x Unidades Produzidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}u00B2}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodos} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}unidades} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Unidades Produzidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{beta} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos x Unidades Produzidas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{n} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodos} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}unidades}
\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{/} \PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}u00B2}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{n} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodos}\PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}
\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}unidades} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{beta}\PY{o}{*}\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodos}
\PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{linspace}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{min}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Períodos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{max}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}hat} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{+} \PY{n}{beta}\PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{x}  
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{A média dos Períodos é: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}periodos}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{A média das Unidades é: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}unidades}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{O valor de Beta é: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{beta}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{O valor de Alpha é: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{alpha}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

MMQ \(\beta\) e \(\alpha\) \[
    \beta = \frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i y_i - (n\cdot\bar{x}\cdot\bar{y})}{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_i^2) - (n\bar{x}^2)}
\]

\[
\alpha = \bar{y}-\beta\cdot \bar{x}
\]

Lembrando que o coeficiente de Pearson é:

\[
r = \mathrm{\frac{cov}{s_x\cdot s_y}}
\]

\[
\mathrm{cov(xy)=\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n\;(x_i-\bar{x})(y_i - \bar{y})}{n}}
\]

e a covariância e o desvio padrão são:

\[
{s_x=\sqrt{\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^n(x_i - \bar{x})^2}{n}}}
\]

            \PY{l+m+mi}{90}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{80}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{90}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{80}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{70}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{90}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{90}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{90}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{60}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{80}\PY{p}{]}

\PY{n}{df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DataFrame}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{\PYZob{}}
    \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Indivíduo}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{21}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}

\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{n} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}idade} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Idade}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}acuidade} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Acuidade}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{p}{)}

    α=101,5 é o intercepto: o ponto em que a reta ajustada cruzaria o eixo
da acuidade, quando a idade fosse zero.\\
Como os dados abrangem apenas idades de 20 a 40 anos, esse valor não tem
interpretação prática direta.\\
Já \(\beta=-0{,}55\) é o coeficiente angular: a cada ano adicional de
idade, a acuidade estimada pela reta diminui, em média, 0,55 unidade.

razoável?\\

    A equação é \(\hat{y}  = 6,7101 -0,2464\cdot x\)

O coeficiente angular é que define a inclinação da reta, podendo ser
positivo ou negativo, resultando em uma reta crescente ou decrescente.\\
O coeficiente linear define o intercepto que é o ponto que a reta cruza
o eixo de x se este estiver dentro do campo visual do gráfico.

    7 -- Um laboratório está interessado em medir o efeito da temperatura
sobre a potência de um antibiótico. Dez amostras de 50 gramas cada foram
guardadas a diferentes temperaturas, e após 15 dias mediu-se a potência.
Os resultados estão na tabela abaixo.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\alph{enumi})}
\tightlist
\item
  Faça a representação gráfica dos dados\\
\item
  Ajuste uma regressão linear, da potência em função da temperatura\\
\item
  A que temperatura a potência média seria nula?
\end{enumerate}

    8 -- Ajuste os dados da tabela abaixo através de uma função potência V =
a Pb onde V representa as vendas, P representa os gastos com promoção e
a e b são dois parâmetros.

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}rr@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
Gastos com promoção (P) & Vendas (V) \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
140 & 50 \\
200 & 57 \\
238 & 67 \\
270 & 65 \\
280 & 66 \\
325 & 70 \\
400 & 77 \\
455 & 79 \\
500 & 80 \\
\end{longtable}
}

Nesse caso vamos aplicar o \(\text{ln(gastos)}\) e ln(vendas) e
acrescentar as colunas de \(\text{gastos}^2\) e de
\(\text{ln gastos}\times\text{ln(vendas)}\) além de calcular as médias
das colunas ln.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{106}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n}{gastos} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{140}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{200}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{238}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{270}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{280}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{325}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{400}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{455}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{500}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{n}{vendas} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{57}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{67}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{65}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{66}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{70}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{77}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{79}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{80}\PY{p}{]}

\PY{n}{ln\PYZus{}gastos} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{log}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{gastos}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{ln\PYZus{}vendas} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{log}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{vendas}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DataFrame}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Gastos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{gastos}\PY{p}{,}
                    \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{ln\PYZus{}gastos}\PY{p}{,}
                    \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Vendas}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{vendas}\PY{p}{,}
                    \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Vendas)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{ln\PYZus{}vendas}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
                \PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos)²}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}
\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos) x ln(Vendas)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Vendas)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}
\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}ln\PYZus{}gastos} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{media\PYZus{}ln\PYZus{}vendas} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Vendas)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{/} \PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ln(Gastos)²}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{n} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{media\PYZus{}ln\PYZus{}gastos}\PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}
\PY{p}{)}
